Delegate bounded implementation or research to fresh-context subagents rather than accumulating their intermediate work in the parent context. ## Output style Be concise. Sacrifice grammar for the sake of concision. show the rest (20,722 more characters)
- Applies to every reply and every file poe writes for the user: `plan.md`, phase briefs, `status.md`, `summary.md`.
- Bullets and fragments over prose.
- Applies to every reply, including errors, failures, and answers to questions; put long content in files.
- Never drop required content: acceptance criteria, risks, verification commands, exact file paths, test results, unknowns.

## Operating modes

- `plan <task>` (or a task description without a mode): write `plan.md` only, then stop so the user can review it. Do not split it into phases and do not edit implementation files.
- `grill <task | plan-path>`: interview the user on open decisions, then write or update `plan.md` (see Grill mode). May write only `poe/<slug>/plan.md`, `poe/docs/GLOSSARY.md`, and `poe/docs/adr/`; never implementation files.
- `split [plan-path]`: split a reviewed `plan.md` into phases and work units, then stop for user approval. Do not edit implementation files.
- `execute [plan-path]`: execute a plan that has been split and approved. If the plan has no phase briefs yet, say so and ask the user to run `split` first.
- When a mode is given no path, use the unambiguous active plan in `poe/` (never `poe/docs/` or `poe/trash/`); otherwise ask for the path.
- `resume [plan-path]`: read `status.md` and the progress notes, compare them with the actual files, tests, and git diff, reconcile discrepancies, and continue only incomplete approved work. When it finishes or stops, write `summary.md`, copy the logs, and build the report as in Execute mode step 9. Task with analyses → also read the latest `analysis/` record and continue the Analyse loop (step 3).
- `report [plan-path]`: copy this session's logs as in the Logs section, build the report as in the Report section, and give the user the path of `report.html`. Do not edit the task's other files or any implementation files.
  Reply: report path + any failure, nothing else.
- `results`: build the results page (see Results page). May write only `poe/docs/all_analysis.html`.
  Reply: page path + any failure, nothing else.
- `analyse [task | plan-path]`: run analyses one at a time on a task, user picks each next one (see Analyse mode). May write `poe/<slug>/` files, `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md`, `poe/docs/all_analysis.html`, and (via workers) notebooks/outputs under the plan's project path; no other implementation files.
- `analyse catch-up`: catch-up check only (see Catch-up check). May write only `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md`, `poe/docs/all_analysis.html`, and `poe:manual` cell tags.
- `note <text>`: log untracked work in the user's words (see Note mode). May write only `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md`, `poe/docs/all_analysis.html`, and `poe:manual` cell tags.
- For small, single-file or tightly coupled changes, recommend doing the work directly instead of forcing subagents. If the user still requests orchestration, comply.

## Files

Everything for one task lives in `poe/<short-task-slug>/`, relative to the current working directory (the directory the session was started in, even if that is not the repository root). Create `poe/` and the task directory if they do not exist (`mkdir -p`). Never overwrite the directory of a different task; pick a distinct slug. Slug `docs` is reserved.

```
poe/docs/              shared across tasks, created only when first needed (written by `grill`, `analyse`, `note`, `results`)
  GLOSSARY.md          project terms
  adr/NNNN-slug.md     architecture decision records
  ANALYSIS_LOG.md      project-wide analysis log, date order, all tasks
  all_analysis.html    results page: every analysis result, project-wide, script-built
poe/<short-task-slug>/
  plan.md              what will be built and why (written by `plan`)
  phases/NN-name.md    one brief per phase, listing its work units (written by `split`)
  analysis/NN-name.md  one record per analysis (written by `analyse` workers)
  status.md            state of every phase and unit, and the next action
  progress/NN-unit.md  running notes written by each worker during execution
  summary.md           execution summary, overwritten at the end of each `execute`/`resume` run
  report.html          step-by-step report built from the files above and logs/
  logs/<session-id>/   copies of the Claude Code transcripts of each session that worked on this task
    <session-id>.jsonl         main (orchestrator) transcript
    subagents/agent-*.jsonl    worker transcripts
    subagents/agent-*.meta.json
```

Notebooks, figures, and intermediates live outside `poe/`, in the project path named in the task's `plan.md` (e.g. `notebooks/<slug>/`); analysis records link to them.

## Plan mode: write `plan.md`

1. Inspect only the relevant repository context, instructions, tests, and environment. Use read-only exploration subagents when exploration would otherwise overwhelm parent context. Also read `poe/docs/` if present.
2. Write `poe/<short-task-slug>/plan.md` from what the user wants to do: objective, scope and exclusions, assumptions, the approach, acceptance criteria, risks, and how the result will be tested or validated. This file describes the work itself; it does not mention phases, subagents, or token budgets. Keep it short: do not restate the request, and give background only where it shapes a decision. Analysis task → plan also gives: question, data paths, notebook/output path, environment (incl. how to call `jupyter`), first analysis (`First analysis`).
3. **Do not overengineer.** Plan the simplest approach that meets the stated requirements:
   - Build only what the user asked for. No speculative features, configuration options, plugin systems, or abstraction layers added "for later".
   - Prefer the tools, libraries, and patterns the repository already uses over introducing new ones.
   - The resulting code must stay readable by humans: plain control flow, descriptive names, small functions, and comments only where the reason is not obvious. Prefer a few clear lines over a clever one.
   - Keep the process as light as the code. Do not add steps, files, or reviews that the task does not need.
4. Open decisions (the user's to make, not settleable from the code or sensible defaults) → do not guess; switch to Grill mode with the task. Otherwise write the plan in one shot.
5. Show a short summary of the plan, state the path of `plan.md`, and stop. Point to the file; do not repeat its contents. The user reviews the file and may ask for changes; apply requested changes to `plan.md` and stop again. Do not proceed to the split until the user runs `split`. Reply terse: ≤5 bullet fragments, no prose, don't repeat file contents; the `Next:` block is not counted. End the reply with a `Next:` line and a fenced block holding the exact command with the full plan path, so it can be pasted into a fresh `/clear`ed session. Nothing after the block:
   ````
   Next:
   ```
   /poe:poe split poe/<short-task-slug>/plan.md
   ```
   ````

## Grill mode: interview, then write `plan.md`

1. Do Plan step 1, then read `poe/docs/` and `/Users/sbandyopadhyay/.claude/plugins/cache/poe-tools/poe/b2550196a669/skills/poe/GRILLING.md`; follow GRILLING.md for questions and writes.
2. Argument is an existing `plan.md` → load it; grill only its assumptions and open or risky points. Otherwise → new slug and a `plan.md` holding only `## Decisions`.
3. Ask one question, then stop. After the answer, write as GRILLING.md says, then ask the next.
4. Frontier empty → ask the user to confirm shared understanding.
5. Confirmed → write or update the full plan (Plan steps 2–3; keep `## Decisions`; link new ADRs), then reply as in Plan step 5.
   `Next:` command: analysis-only task → `/poe:poe analyse <plan path>`; coding or mixed → `split <plan path>`, mixed adds one reply bullet: `analyse <plan path>` works after execute.
6. Copy the logs (see Logs) at the end of the mode.

## Split mode: split `plan.md` into phases and work units

Read `plan.md` as it currently is on disk, including any edits the user made by hand. If phase briefs already exist from an earlier split and no unit has been started, replace them; if work has started, ask before changing them.

1. Split the plan into ordered **phases** with explicit dependencies, and each phase into **work units**. One work unit is handled by one fresh subagent and must be independently verifiable.
2. Size each unit so that one subagent can finish it in roughly 100k tokens of context, counting the files it must read, the edits, and the test output. Split a unit that looks larger; merge units that are trivially small. Use as few phases and units as the work actually needs.
3. Identify which units can run in parallel because they do not modify overlapping files or share mutable state. Run dependent or overlapping units sequentially, or isolate them in separate git worktrees and integrate deliberately.
4. Write one brief per phase at `phases/NN-name.md`. For each unit give: goal, inputs and files to read, files it may change, explicit non-goals, exact verification commands, completion criteria, and its progress-notes path. When `poe/docs/GLOSSARY.md` or relevant `poe/docs/adr/` files exist, list them in each unit's inputs. Analysis unit → brief also gives notebook path + record path; units that write `ANALYSIS_LOG.md` run sequentially.
5. Write `status.md` listing every phase and unit as pending.
6. Show the phase split, state where the files are, and ask for approval. Point to the files; do not repeat their contents. Do not start implementation in `split` mode. Reply terse: ≤5 bullet fragments, no prose, don't repeat file contents; the `Next:` block is not counted. End the reply with the same `Next:` line and fenced block as Plan mode step 5, holding the execute command. Nothing after the block:
   ````
   Next:
   ```
   /poe:poe execute poe/<short-task-slug>/plan.md
   ```
   ````

## Execute mode

1. Read `plan.md`, `status.md`, and the brief of the next phase. Verify that the plan is approved and the prerequisites of the next unit are satisfied.
2. Dispatch a **fresh non-fork subagent** for each work unit. Prefer the `poe:phase-worker` subagent bundled with this plugin (if it is not available under that name, look for `phase-worker`); otherwise use an appropriate built-in agent capable of doing the work. Do not use a fork for routine delegation: it inherits the parent's history.
3. Give the worker only: the goal, the phase-brief path, necessary repository paths, interfaces and dependencies, allowed files, completion criteria, and its progress-notes path. Do not paste the parent conversation or large tool output. Analysis unit → also notebook path, record path, log path; worker follows Notebook rules and writes the record + log entry besides its progress notes; log-writing units run one at a time.
4. The worker implements or analyzes only its assigned unit and runs the relevant tests. It writes its progress notes **as it works**, not only at the end: it updates the notes file after each meaningful step, so that the notes are current if the worker stops, is compacted, or fails.
5. Run independent, non-overlapping units in parallel only when safe. Never silently overwrite another agent's edits.
6. Check the worker's claims against the actual files, test outputs, and git diff. For significant or high-risk changes, use a separate fresh-context reviewer before marking the unit verified. Fix or reassign failed units with a new scoped brief.
7. Update `status.md` after every unit. Keep the parent response concise: phase, tests, blockers, and next action. Reference files rather than returning large transcripts or repeating file contents.
   Reply terse: ≤5 bullet fragments, no prose, don't repeat file contents.
8. Move to the next approved phase after verification. Stop for genuinely ambiguous requirements, security-sensitive changes, destructive operations, deployments, or decisions beyond the approved plan.
9. At completion, run integration tests, review the full change set, and report what passed, what remains uncertain, and where the files were saved; point to files rather than repeating their contents. Write the same report to `summary.md` (what passed, what remains uncertain, blockers, and file locations), overwriting any earlier one; `status.md` stays the source of truth for state. Then copy the logs (see Logs) and build the report (see Report).
   Reply terse: ≤10 bullet fragments, no prose, don't repeat file contents.

## Analyse mode

One analysis at a time; user picks each next one. `NN` continues across `analyse` runs within a task.

1. No plan → as Plan step 4: open decisions → Grill mode with the task; else write short `plan.md` (Plan step 2 analysis fields). Either way stop for review as Plan step 5, `Next:` block holding `/poe:poe analyse <plan path>`.
   Plan exists, analysis fields (Plan step 2) missing → look up facts; grill the rest (GRILLING.md method, `## Decisions` appended); write fields into `plan.md`; continue. Else → continue.
2. Catch-up check (below).
3. Dispatch fresh `phase-worker` (as Execute step 2) with: question for this analysis (first: plan's `First analysis`; later: user's pick), notebook path, record path `analysis/NN-name.md`, previous record path, log path `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md`.
4. Worker: add tagged cells under a markdown heading `## NN: <question>`, execute, write record, append log entry, return ≤5 bullets + 2–3 proposed next analyses.
5. Orchestrator: verify notebook executed without error (no `"output_type": "error"`) and record exists; update `status.md`; rebuild results page (see Results page); ask next analysis via `AskUserQuestion` (worker's options + done); write the chosen option into the record's `## Next`; repeat from 3.
6. Need a tool → `plan`/`split`/`execute` on same task dir; phases and analyses share `status.md`. Back → `analyse` again.
7. User says done → `summary.md`, logs, report as in Execute step 9.

## Analysis record

`analysis/NN-name.md`, written by the worker:

```
# NN: <question>: status (done | failed | abandoned)
## Follows from
## What was run        notebook path + heading, inputs
## Result              numbers, figure/table paths
## Interpretation
## Caveats
## Next                options proposed; option chosen
```

## Analysis log entry

Appended to `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md` (create with `# Analysis log` heading if absent):

```
## YYYY-MM-DD: <question>  [<slug>/NN | manual]
- Result: one line
- By: poe | user | other agent
- Follows: <slug>/NN
- Links: record, notebook#heading, figures
```

## Notebook rules

- Agent-written cells tagged in cell metadata: `tags: ["poe:<slug>/<NN>"]`. Untagged code cells = untracked work.
- Default: append to the task's existing notebook. Human reads all of it: fewest files that keep it organised and re-runs fast.
- New notebook only when: full re-run exceeds ~5 min, or question changes topic. Then save intermediates the next notebook loads; state the reason in the record.
- No dead cells: failed attempts removed from notebook, described in record.
- Execute headless: `jupyter nbconvert --to notebook --execute --inplace <nb>`, in the plan's environment; `jupyter` missing → report and stop. Notebook saved by someone else since the worker read it → warn, don't overwrite.

## Catch-up check

- Runs at start of every `analyse`; also `analyse catch-up` alone.
- Scan notebooks under the plan's notebook/output path for untagged code cells; a notebook with no tagged cells at all counts as one group.
- Per group of adjacent untagged cells: draft `manual` log entry from code + outputs, ask user to confirm/fix wording, append. Tag cells `poe:manual` so they are not re-asked.
- `Follows: <slug>/NN` set when group sits right after a `poe:<slug>/<NN>` group or user names it; else `-`.
- After log writes: rebuild results page (see Results page).

## Note mode

- Append `manual` log entry: user's text as `<question>`, `Result` only if the text states one; link notebook if named or unambiguous; tag matching untagged cells `poe:manual`. No worker. Reply: entry path only.
- `Follows: <slug>/NN` set when the tagged cells sit right after a `poe:<slug>/<NN>` group or user names it; else `-`.
- After log write: rebuild results page (see Results page).
- Logs copied into the active task if unambiguous; otherwise skipped.

## Logs

At the end of every mode (`plan`, `grill`, `split`, `execute`, `resume`, `report`, `results`, `analyse`, `note`), and whenever execution stops for user input, copy this session's transcripts into the task directory:

```sh
dest="poe/<short-task-slug>/logs/3b7ba072-0ca9-4e04-ad19-bbe2f0660b57"
src=$(find "${CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}/projects" -name "3b7ba072-0ca9-4e04-ad19-bbe2f0660b57.jsonl" | head -1)
mkdir -p "$dest"
cp "$src" "$dest/"
[ -d "${src%.jsonl}/subagents" ] && cp -R "${src%.jsonl}/subagents" "$dest/"
```

- Re-running it overwrites the earlier copy with the current, longer transcript, so repeating it is safe.
- The copy is a snapshot: anything after it (such as the final reply of the turn) is missing until the next copy.
- If the transcript cannot be found (`src` is empty or `cp` fails), say so in the reply and carry on. A missing log never blocks the task.

## Report

At the end of `execute` and `resume` (after `summary.md` and the log copy), and in `report` mode, build `report.html` from the task directory:

```sh
python3 "/Users/sbandyopadhyay/.claude/plugins/cache/poe-tools/poe/b2550196a669/skills/poe/scripts/poe_report.py" poe/<short-task-slug>
```

- The script writes `poe/<short-task-slug>/report.html` and prints its path. Re-running it replaces the earlier report.
- If it fails (for example, `python3` is missing or the command is refused), say so in the reply and carry on. A missing report never blocks the task.

## Results page

In `results` mode, and after log writes in Analyse step 5, Note mode, and Catch-up check, build the results page from `poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md` and every task's `analysis/` records:

```sh
python3 "/Users/sbandyopadhyay/.claude/plugins/cache/poe-tools/poe/b2550196a669/skills/poe/scripts/poe_results.py" .
```

- Writes `poe/docs/all_analysis.html` (creates it if absent), prints its path. Re-running replaces it.
- Fails → say so in the reply and carry on. A missing results page never blocks the task.
- `results` mode: logs copied into the active task if unambiguous; otherwise skipped.

## Progress notes

Each worker keeps one file, `progress/NN-unit.md`, and updates it throughout its run. `resume` and any follow-on worker rely on it, so it must make sense to an agent that has seen nothing else.

```
# Unit ID: status (in progress | done | partial | blocked)
## Done so far
## Files changed or produced
## Tests run and observed results
## Decisions and assumptions
## Next step / remaining work
```

When a unit is handed to a second worker, that worker reads the existing notes and continues in the same file.

## Context policy: about 100k tokens per agent

- 100k is a **soft target**, not a hard limit. Never claim to know exact token usage unless a tool reports it; otherwise judge by how much has been read and produced.
- Hand over at a natural boundary, not at a number. When a worker nears the target, it finishes the step it is in the middle of (an edit in flight, a test run, a debugging thread that is about to resolve), then brings its progress notes up to date and returns `partial`. Going moderately over the target to finish a coherent step is correct; stopping mid-edit to respect the number is not.
- The orchestrator then starts a **fresh non-fork worker** for the remainder, pointed at the progress notes. Do not keep resuming a bloated agent.
- Prefer this file-based handover to automatic compaction. Automatic compaction is a backstop: it can fire in the middle of a step and loses detail, which is why the notes are kept current throughout.
- Keep the parent lean: orchestration and short summaries only; delegate large searches, code reading, and log interpretation.
- Avoid unnecessary parallelism, repeated repository scans, duplicate reading, and verbose inter-agent messages.

## Constraints

- Follow the project's `CLAUDE.md`, security practices, and user instructions.
- Implement what the plan says and no more. Keep code simple and readable by humans; do not add abstractions or features the plan does not call for.
- Preserve existing user changes; check git status before edits. Do not commit, push, merge, delete, or deploy without authorization.
- Never infer that a test passed without observing its result. Preserve unknowns rather than fabricating evidence.
- If an agent has no delegation tool, say so and execute sequentially using the same file-based phase structure.

#102 · 2026-11-20T22:34:19.122Z · tool result · Read
490 491 LIST_ITEM = re.compile(r"^(\s*)([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$") 492 TABLE_SEPARATOR = re.compile(r"^\s*\|?\s*:?-+:?\s*(\|\s*:?-+:?\s*)*\|?\s*$")show the rest (8,263 more characters)
493	INLINE_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
494	INLINE_LINK = re.compile(r"!?\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)")
495	BOLD = re.compile(r"\*\*(?=\S)(.+?)(?<=\S)\*\*")
496	ITALIC = re.compile(r"\*(?=[^\s*])([^*]*?)(?<=[^\s*])\*")
497	URL_SCHEME = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*:")
498	PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\x00(\d+)\x00")
499	NESTED_INDENT = 2  # a line indented this much past a list item belongs to that item
500	
501	
502	def link_href(target: str) -> Optional[str]:
503	    """Href for a markdown link target; None for a scheme other than http(s) or mailto."""
504	    if target.startswith("#"):
505	        return target
506	    if URL_SCHEME.match(target):
507	        if target.lower().startswith(("http://", "https://", "mailto:")):
508	            return target
509	        return None
510	    path, hash_sign, fragment = target.partition("#")
511	    return page_href(path) + hash_sign + quote(fragment)
512	
513	
514	def render_inline(text: str) -> str:
515	    """Escape one line of text, then render `code`, [links](target), **bold** and *italic*.
516	
517	    Code spans and links are rendered first and parked as numbered
518	    placeholders, so bold and italic never reach inside them.
519	    """
520	    rendered_pieces: List[str] = []
521	
522	    def park(rendered: str) -> str:
523	        rendered_pieces.append(rendered)
524	        return f"\x00{len(rendered_pieces) - 1}\x00"
525	
526	    def restore(text_with_placeholders: str) -> str:
527	        return PLACEHOLDER.sub(lambda match: rendered_pieces[int(match.group(1))], text_with_placeholders)
528	
529	    def emphasis(escaped: str) -> str:
530	        escaped = BOLD.sub(r"<strong>\1</strong>", escaped)
531	        return ITALIC.sub(r"<em>\1</em>", escaped)
532	
533	    def code(match) -> str:
534	        return park(f"<code>{escape(match.group(1))}</code>")
535	
536	    def link(match) -> str:
537	        label = restore(emphasis(escape(match.group(1)))) or escape(match.group(2))
538	        href = link_href(match.group(2))
539	        if href is None:
540	            return park(label)
541	        return park(f'<a href="{escape(href)}">{label}</a>')
542	
543	    text = text.replace("\x00", "")
544	    text = INLINE_CODE.sub(code, text)
545	    text = INLINE_LINK.sub(link, text)
546	    return restore(emphasis(escape(text)))
547	
548	
549	def indent_of(line: str) -> int:
550	    return len(line) - len(line.lstrip())
551	
552	
553	def dedent(lines: List[str]) -> List[str]:
554	    """Remove the indent all non-blank lines share."""
555	    indents = [indent_of(line) for line in lines if line.strip()]
556	    common = min(indents) if indents else 0
557	    return [line[common:] if line.strip() else "" for line in lines]
558	
559	
560	def is_table_start(lines: List[str], index: int) -> bool:
561	    """A pipe table starts with a header row followed by a `|---|---|` row."""
562	    if index + 1 >= len(lines):
563	        return False
564	    header, separator = lines[index], lines[index + 1]
565	    return "|" in header and "|" in separator and bool(TABLE_SEPARATOR.match(separator))
566	
567	
568	def table_cells(line: str) -> List[str]:
569	    row = line.strip()
570	    if row.startswith("|"):
571	        row = row[1:]
572	    if row.endswith("|"):
573	        row = row[:-1]
574	    return [cell.strip() for cell in row.split("|")]
575	
576	
577	def render_table(lines: List[str], start: int) -> Tuple[str, int]:
578	    """Render the table starting at lines[start]; return (html, index after it)."""
579	    header = table_cells(lines[start])
580	    index = start + 2  # skip the separator row
581	    rows = []
582	    while index < len(lines) and lines[index].strip() and "|" in lines[index]:
583	        rows.append(table_cells(lines[index]))
584	        index += 1
585	    head = "".join(f"<th>{render_inline(cell)}</th>" for cell in header)
586	    body = []
587	    for row in rows:
588	        row = row + [""] * (len(header) - len(row))
589	        body.append("<tr>" + "".join(f"<td>{render_inline(cell)}</td>" for cell in row) + "</tr>")
590	    return (
591	        f'<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>{head}</tr></thead>'
592	        f"<tbody>{''.join(body)}</tbody></table></div>",
593	        index,
594	    )
595	
596	
597	def render_paragraph(lines: List[str], start: int) -> Tuple[str, int]:
598	    """Render lines up to a blank line, list item or table; line breaks are kept."""
599	    index = start
600	    paragraph = []
601	    while index < len(lines):
602	        line = lines[index]
603	        if not line.strip() or (index > start and (LIST_ITEM.match(line) or is_table_start(lines, index))):
604	            break
605	        paragraph.append(render_inline(line.strip()))
606	        index += 1
607	    return f"<p>{'<br>'.join(paragraph)}</p>", index
608	
609	
610	def render_list(lines: List[str], start: int) -> Tuple[str, int]:
611	    """Render the list starting at lines[start]; return (html, index after it).
612	
613	    Lines indented at least NESTED_INDENT past the list's items belong to the
614	    item above them (sub-lists, tables, more text) and are rendered as blocks
615	    inside it.
616	    """
617	    first_item = LIST_ITEM.match(lines[start])
618	    list_indent = len(first_item.group(1))
619	    ordered = first_item.group(2)[0].isdigit()
620	
621	    def is_sibling_item(line: str) -> bool:
622	        item = LIST_ITEM.match(line)
623	        return bool(
624	            item
625	            and list_indent <= len(item.group(1)) < list_indent + NESTED_INDENT
626	            and item.group(2)[0].isdigit() == ordered
627	        )
628	
629	    items: List[Tuple[List[str], List[str]]] = []  # (item text lines, nested lines)
630	    index = start
631	    while index < len(lines):
632	        line = lines[index]
633	        if not line.strip():
634	            next_index = index + 1
635	            while next_index < len(lines) and not lines[next_index].strip():
636	                next_index += 1
637	            if next_index == len(lines):
638	                break
639	            next_line = lines[next_index]
640	            if indent_of(next_line) >= list_indent + NESTED_INDENT:
641	                items[-1][1].append("")
642	            elif not is_sibling_item(next_line):
643	                break
644	            index = next_index
645	        elif indent_of(line) >= list_indent + NESTED_INDENT:
646	            items[-1][1].append(line)
647	            index += 1
648	        elif is_sibling_item(line):
649	            items.append(([LIST_ITEM.match(line).group(3)], []))
650	            index += 1
651	        elif LIST_ITEM.match(line) or is_table_start(lines, index) or items[-1][1]:
652	            break
653	        else:  # unindented text right under an item continues its text
654	            items[-1][0].append(line.strip())
655	            index += 1
656	
657	    rendered_items = []
658	    for text_lines, nested_lines in items:
659	        nested = dedent(nested_lines)
660	        while nested and nested[0].strip() and not LIST_ITEM.match(nested[0]) and not is_table_start(nested, 0):
661	            text_lines.append(nested.pop(0).strip())
662	        text = render_inline(" ".join(text_lines))
663	        rendered_items.append(f"<li>{text}{render_blocks(nested)}</li>")
664	    tag = "ol" if ordered else "ul"
665	    return f"<{tag}>{''.join(rendered_items)}</{tag}>", index
666	
667	
668	def render_blocks(lines: List[str]) -> str:
669	    parts = []
670	    index = 0
671	    while index < len(lines):
672	        if not lines[index].strip():
673	            index += 1
674	            continue
675	        if is_table_start(lines, index):
676	            rendered, index = render_table(lines, index)
677	        elif LIST_ITEM.match(lines[index]):
678	            rendered, index = render_list(lines, index)
679	        else:
680	            rendered, index = render_paragraph(lines, index)
681	        parts.append(rendered)
682	    return "".join(parts)
683	
684	
685	def render_markdown(text: str) -> str:
686	    """Render record markdown (the subset above) as HTML; never raises."""
687	    lines = [line.expandtabs(4).rstrip() for line in text.replace("\x00", "").splitlines()]
688	    try:
689	        return render_blocks(lines)
#103 · 2026-11-20T22:34:24.095Z · thinking

I also need a small CSS rule for h4 spacing in the result and interpretation areas, and I still have to find where the styles live.

# results-md-figures

## Decisions
- Results page renders record markdown via small stdlib subset renderer in `poe_results.py` (paragraphs, nested bullets, tables, bold/italic, inline code, links); replaces escaped pre-wrap text. Why: page stays stdlib-only, no JS, self-contained.
- Figure size: default 4x3 in per single plot; small/square ≤3.5 in wide (e.g. scatter 3.5x3.5); multi-panel ≤7.2 in wide (double column); never larger. Why: plots drop into journal panels without resizing.
- Figure font: Arial 7 pt at final size; `svg.fonttype='none'` keeps text editable. Why: journal-typical size; Arial present in Inkscape almost everywhere.
- Figure rules live in Notebook rules (SKILL.md + phase-worker.md): one rcParams setup cell at notebook top (snippet given), figsize per plot, save `.svg` only. Why: notebook self-contained, reruns anywhere; no plugin-path dependency.
- Confirmed by user 2026-11-20.

## Objective

- Results page (`poe/docs/all_analysis.html`) renders record markdown properly (screenshot: literal `**`, `- `, `| table |`).
- Every analysis plot is paper-panel-ready: SVG, editable text, panel size.

## Scope

- `skills/poe/scripts/poe_results.py`: stdlib markdown-subset renderer for `## Result` and Interpretation; CSS for lists, tables, code.
- `tests/test_poe_results.py` + `tests/fixtures/results/`: record with nested bullets, table, bold, code, link.
- `skills/poe/SKILL.md` Notebook rules + `agents/phase-worker.md`: figure rules + setup-cell snippet; Analyse step 5 also checks figure paths end `.svg`.
- `README.md`: one line on figure rules, if README lists notebook rules.

Excluded:
- Converting existing PNGs or re-running old notebooks (forward only).
- Full CommonMark (headings inside sections, blockquotes, HTML passthrough, footnotes).
- Shipped `.mplstyle` / helper module; non-matplotlib libs beyond "same rules apply".
- Changes to `poe_report.py`.

## Assumptions

- Markdown subset: paragraphs; `-`/`*`/`1.` lists nested by indent (2+ spaces); pipe tables with `---` separator row; `**bold**`, `*italic*`, `` `code` ``, `[text](url)`. Anything else → escaped text in a paragraph. Everything escaped before inline markup applied (no HTML injection).
- Link targets that are session-relative paths → rewritten via existing `page_href`; `http(s)` kept.
- Interpretation: still first block only (paragraph or list), rendered with same renderer.
- Figures detection unchanged (`.svg` already an image extension); SVG inlined via `<img>`, `max-width: 100%`.
- Setup cell (top of notebook, tagged like other poe cells):
  ```python
  import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  plt.rcParams.update({
      "svg.fonttype": "none",
      "font.family": "sans-serif",
      "font.sans-serif": ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"],
      "font.size": 7,
      "axes.titlesize": 7, "axes.labelsize": 7,
      "xtick.labelsize": 7, "ytick.labelsize": 7, "legend.fontsize": 7,
      "figure.figsize": (4, 3),
  })
  ```
- Save: `fig.savefig("<name>.svg", bbox_inches="tight")`; no PNG. Dense artists (scatters ≳10k points, images) `rasterized=True`.
- Size caps: default 4x3 in; small/square ≤3.5 in wide (e.g. 3.5x3.5); multi-panel ≤7.2 in wide; never larger. Set per plot via `figsize`.
- seaborn/pandas plotting inherit rcParams → covered. Plotly etc. not used by default; if used, same SVG/size/font rules in spirit.
- Existing notebook lacking setup cell → worker adds it at top when it next appends.

## Approach

- `poe_results.py`: add `render_markdown(text, session_dir) -> str` (block pass: tables, lists via indent stack, paragraphs) + `render_inline(text)` (escape, then code → bold → italic → links). Replace `<div class="result">{escape(...)}` and interpretation `<p>` with it. Drop `white-space: pre-wrap`; add CSS for `ul/ol`, `table` (borders, padding, `overflow-x:auto` wrapper), `code`.
- SKILL.md Notebook rules: new bullets: figures SVG only; setup cell snippet; size caps; rasterize dense artists. Keep terse.
- phase-worker.md: same rules (or point to SKILL.md Notebook rules if worker already reads them); change example `fig.png` → `fig.svg`.

## Acceptance criteria

- Fixture record with nested bullets, table, bold, code, link renders as `<ul>` nesting, `<table>`, `<strong>`, `<code>`, `<a>`; no literal `**` or `|` row text in output.
- `<script>` in a record → escaped, not emitted raw.
- Byte-identical output on rerun; existing tests pass (update ones asserting old pre-wrap output).
- SKILL.md + phase-worker.md state: SVG only, `svg.fonttype: none`, Arial 7 pt, size caps, setup cell snippet.
- Manual: rebuild page on a real project with the screenshot record (`FLLQHLPLV`) → bullets/table render cleanly, light + dark.

## Risks

- Agent-written markdown is loose (odd indents, tables without leading `|`) → renderer misparses. Mitigation: tolerant parse; fallback = escaped paragraph, never crash.
- Arial missing on cluster/Linux → matplotlib falls back to DejaVu with warning; SVG still names Arial-first stack, Inkscape on Mac uses Arial. Accepted.
- Workers ignore rules → plots still big/PNG. Mitigation: rule in both SKILL.md and phase-worker.md; orchestrator Analyse step 5 checks figure paths end `.svg`.

## Validation

- `python3 -m unittest discover tests`
- `python3 skills/poe/scripts/poe_results.py tests/fixtures/results` → open page, check rendering light + dark.
- Grep: `grep -n "svg.fonttype" skills/poe/SKILL.md agents/phase-worker.md`.

1 #!/usr/bin/env python3 2 """Build the project-wide results page from the analysis log and records. 3 show the rest (26,822 more characters)
     4	Usage: python3 poe_results.py [session-dir]     (default: .)
     5	
     6	Three parts: Load reads poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md and every
     7	poe/<slug>/analysis/NN-*.md (except under poe/docs/ and poe/trash/), Arrange
     8	groups the analyses into one section per task, nested by their `Follows`
     9	chain, and places untracked (`manual`) log entries, and Render writes
    10	<session-dir>/poe/docs/all_analysis.html (inline CSS, no JavaScript, no
    11	external resources, no dates, so the same input gives the same bytes).
    12	Python 3 standard library only.
    13	
    14	Records and log entries are free-form markdown, so only headings and the
    15	fixed log bullet keys are parsed. A record without a `# NN: <question>`
    16	title and a log heading that does not match the entry format are skipped
    17	and counted in the page footer, never fatal.
    18	"""
    19	
    20	import argparse
    21	import html
    22	import re
    23	import sys
    24	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    25	from pathlib import Path
    26	from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    27	from urllib.parse import quote
    28	
    29	LOG_PATH = Path("poe") / "docs" / "ANALYSIS_LOG.md"
    30	PAGE_PATH = Path("poe") / "docs" / "all_analysis.html"
    31	SKIPPED_TASK_DIRS = {"docs", "trash"}
    32	
    33	IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".svg", ".gif"}
    34	# Extensions that make a token a file path even without a "/" in it.
    35	FILE_EXTENSIONS = IMAGE_EXTENSIONS | {
    36	    ".csv", ".tsv", ".pdf", ".html", ".txt", ".json", ".xlsx", ".parquet", ".ipynb", ".md", ".py",
    37	}
    38	RECORD_STATUSES = {"done", "failed", "abandoned"}
    39	DIMMED_STATUSES = {"failed", "abandoned"}
    40	
    41	RECORD_NAME = re.compile(r"^(\d+)-.*\.md$")
    42	RECORD_TITLE = re.compile(r"^#\s*(\d+)\s*:\s*(.+?)\s*$")
    43	LOG_HEADING = re.compile(r"^##\s+\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\s*:\s*(.+?)\s*\[\s*([^\]]+?)\s*\]\s*$")
    44	LOG_BULLET = re.compile(r"^-\s*(Result|By|Follows|Links)\s*:\s*(.*?)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
    45	ANALYSIS_REF = re.compile(r"^([A-Za-z0-9][\w.-]*)/(\d+)$")
    46	PATH_TOKEN = re.compile(r"^[\w.~/-]+\.[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*$")
    47	MARKDOWN_LINK_TARGET = re.compile(r"\]\(([^)\s]+)\)")
    48	BACKTICK_TEXT = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
    49	
    50	
    51	@dataclass
    52	class Analysis:
    53	    """One analysis shown on the page: a poe record or an untracked log entry."""
    54	
    55	    key: str  # "<slug>/<NN>" for poe analyses, "manual-<k>" for untracked work
    56	    anchor: str
    57	    question: str
    58	    slug: Optional[str] = None  # None for untracked work
    59	    number: Optional[int] = None
    60	    status: str = ""
    61	    result: str = ""
    62	    interpretation: str = ""
    63	    follows: Optional[str] = None  # "<slug>/<NN>"
    64	    by: str = ""
    65	    record: Optional[str] = None  # paths relative to the session dir
    66	    notebook: Optional[str] = None
    67	    figures: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    68	    files: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    69	    log_index: int = 0  # position in the log, orders untracked work
    70	    is_latest: bool = False
    71	    children: List["Analysis"] = field(default_factory=list)
    72	
    73	
    74	@dataclass
    75	class LogEntry:
    76	    index: int
    77	    question: str
    78	    ref: str  # "<slug>/<NN>" or "manual"
    79	    result: str = ""
    80	    by: str = ""
    81	    follows: Optional[str] = None
    82	    links: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    83

    86	class TaskSection:
    87	    slug: str
    88	    roots: List[Analysis] = field(default_factory=list)
    89

    92	class Page:
    93	    tasks: List[TaskSection] = field(default_factory=list)
    94	    untracked: List[Analysis] = field(default_factory=list)

    97	    entry_count: int = 0

   100	
   101	# ------------------------------------------------------------------- parsing
   102	
   103	
   104	def read_text(path: Path) -> str:
   105	    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   106	
   107	
   108	def analysis_ref(text: str) -> Optional[str]:
   109	    """Return "<slug>/<NN>" (NN two digits or more) for a "slug/NN" text, else None."""
   110	    match = ANALYSIS_REF.match(text.strip().strip("`"))
   111	    if not match:
   112	        return None
   113	    return f"{match.group(1)}/{int(match.group(2)):02d}"
   114	
   115	
   116	def looks_like_path(token: str) -> bool:
   117	    if not PATH_TOKEN.match(token):
   118	        return False
   119	    return "/" in token or Path(token).suffix.lower() in FILE_EXTENSIONS
   120	
   121	
   122	def extract_paths(text: str) -> List[str]:
   123	    """Return the file paths in text, in order, without duplicates.
   124	
   125	    A path may be written plain, in backticks, or as a markdown link target.
   126	    A "#heading" after a path (notebook#heading) is dropped.
   127	    """
   128	    candidates = MARKDOWN_LINK_TARGET.findall(text)
   129	    candidates += BACKTICK_TEXT.findall(text)
   130	    candidates += text.split()
   131	    paths = []
   132	    for candidate in candidates:
   133	        token = candidate.split("#")[0].strip("`*_'\"()[]<>,;:!")
   134	        token = token.rstrip(".")
   135	        if looks_like_path(token) and token not in paths:
   136	            paths.append(token)
   137	    return paths
   138	
   139	
   140	def split_sections(text: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
   141	    """Map each lowercased `## heading` to the text under it."""
   142	    sections: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
   143	    current = None
   144	    for line in text.splitlines():
   145	        if line.startswith("## "):
   146	            current = line[3:].strip().lower()
   147	            sections[current] = []
   148	        elif current is not None:
   149	            sections[current].append(line)
   150	    return {name: "\n".join(lines).strip() for name, lines in sections.items()}
   151	
   152	
   153	def section(sections: Dict[str, str], name: str) -> str:
   154	    """Text of the first section whose heading starts with name."""
   155	    for heading, text in sections.items():
   156	        if heading.startswith(name):
   157	            return text
   158	    return ""
   159	
   160	
   161	def first_paragraph(text: str) -> str:
   162	    lines = []
   163	    for line in text.strip().splitlines():
   164	        if not line.strip():
   165	            break
   166	        lines.append(line.strip())
   167	    return " ".join(lines)
   168	
   169	
   170	def parse_follows_from(text: str, slug: str) -> Optional[str]:
   171	    """Read a record's `Follows from`: "slug/NN", a record path, or a bare NN of the same task."""
   172	    for token in text.replace(",", " ").split():
   173	        token = token.strip("`*_'\"()[]<>.;:")
   174	        path_match = re.search(r"(?:poe/([\w.-]+)/)?analysis/(\d+)-", token)
   175	        if path_match:
   176	            return f"{path_match.group(1) or slug}/{int(path_match.group(2)):02d}"
   177	        ref = analysis_ref(token)
   178	        if ref:
   179	            return ref
   180	    bare_number = re.match(r"^\W*(\d+)\b", text)
   181	    if bare_number:
   182	        return f"{slug}/{int(bare_number.group(1)):02d}"
   183	    return None
   184	
   185	
   186	def split_paths(paths: List[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], List[str], List[str]]:
   187	    """Sort paths into (record, notebook, figures, other files)."""
   188	    record = notebook = None
   189	    figures, files = [], []
   190	    for path in paths:
   191	        suffix = Path(path).suffix.lower()
   192	        if suffix == ".md" and record is None:
   193	            record = path
   194	        elif suffix == ".ipynb" and notebook is None:
   195	            notebook = path
   196	        elif suffix in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS:
   197	            figures.append(path)
   198	        elif suffix not in (".md", ".ipynb"):
   199	            files.append(path)
   200	    return record, notebook, figures, files
   201	
   202	
   203	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- load
   204	
   205	
   206	def load_record(path: Path, slug: str, number: int) -> Optional[Analysis]:
   207	    """Parse one analysis record; None if it has no `# NN: <question>` title."""
   208	    text = read_text(path)
   209	    title = next((line for line in text.splitlines() if line.startswith("# ")), "")
   210	    match = RECORD_TITLE.match(title)
   211	    if not match:
   212	        return None
   213	    question, status = match.group(2), ""
   214	    if ":" in question:
   215	        before, after = question.rsplit(":", 1)
   216	        if after.strip().lower() in RECORD_STATUSES:
   217	            question, status = before.strip(), after.strip().lower()
   218	
   219	    sections = split_sections(text)
   220	    _, notebook, _, _ = split_paths(extract_paths(section(sections, "what was run")))
   221	    result = section(sections, "result")
   222	    _, _, figures, files = split_paths(extract_paths(result))
   223	    key = f"{slug}/{number:02d}"
   224	    return Analysis(
   225	        key=key,
   226	        anchor=f"analysis-{slug}-{number:02d}",
   227	        question=question,
   228	        slug=slug,
   229	        number=number,
   230	        status=status,
   231	        result=result,
   232	        interpretation=first_paragraph(section(sections, "interpretation")),
   233	        follows=parse_follows_from(section(sections, "follows from"), slug),
   234	        record=f"poe/{slug}/analysis/{path.name}",
   235	        notebook=notebook,
   236	        figures=figures,
   237	        files=files,
   238	    )
   239	
   240	
   241	def load_records(session_dir: Path) -> Tuple[List[Analysis], int]:
   242	    """Return every task's analysis records, by slug then NN, and the count skipped."""
   243	    analyses, skipped = [], 0
   244	    poe_dir = session_dir / "poe"
   245	    if not poe_dir.is_dir():
   246	        return analyses, skipped
   247	    for task_dir in sorted(poe_dir.iterdir()):
   248	        if task_dir.name in SKIPPED_TASK_DIRS or not (task_dir / "analysis").is_dir():
   249	            continue
   250	        for path in sorted((task_dir / "analysis").glob("*.md")):
   251	            name_match = RECORD_NAME.match(path.name)
   252	            if not name_match:
   253	                continue
   254	            try:
   255	                analysis = load_record(path, task_dir.name, int(name_match.group(1)))
   256	            except OSError:
   257	                analysis = None
   258	            if analysis is None:
   259	                skipped += 1
   260	            else:
   261	                analyses.append(analysis)
   262	    analyses.sort(key=lambda analysis: (analysis.slug, analysis.number))
   263	    return analyses, skipped
   264	
   265	
   266	def load_log(session_dir: Path) -> Tuple[List[LogEntry], int]:
   267	    """Return the log entries in log order and the count of malformed headings."""
   268	    log_path = session_dir / LOG_PATH
   269	    if not log_path.is_file():
   270	        return [], 0
   271	    entries, skipped = [], 0
   272	    entry = None
   273	    for line in read_text(log_path).splitlines():
   274	        if line.startswith("## "):
   275	            match = LOG_HEADING.match(line)
   276	            if match:
   277	                entry = LogEntry(index=len(entries) + 1, question=match.group(1), ref=match.group(2))
   278	                entries.append(entry)
   279	            else:
   280	                entry = None
   281	                skipped += 1
   282	            continue
   283	        bullet = LOG_BULLET.match(line.strip())
   284	        if entry is None or not bullet:
   285	            continue
   286	        key, value = bullet.group(1).lower(), bullet.group(2)
   287	        if key == "result":
   288	            entry.result = value
   289	        elif key == "by":
   290	            entry.by = value
   291	        elif key == "follows":
   292	            entry.follows = analysis_ref(value)
   293	        elif key == "links":
   294	            entry.links = extract_paths(value)
   295	    return entries, skipped
   296	
   297	
   298	# ------------------------------------------------------------------- arrange
   299	
   300	
   301	def analysis_from_log(entry: LogEntry) -> Analysis:
   302	    record, notebook, figures, files = split_paths(entry.links)
   303	    ref = analysis_ref(entry.ref)
   304	    if ref:
   305	        slug, number = ref.split("/")
   306	        key, anchor = ref, f"analysis-{slug}-{number}"
   307	        slug_or_none, number_or_none = slug, int(number)
   308	    else:
   309	        key, anchor = f"manual-{entry.index}", f"manual-{entry.index}"
   310	        slug_or_none = number_or_none = None
   311	    return Analysis(
   312	        key=key,
   313	        anchor=anchor,
   314	        question=entry.question,
   315	        slug=slug_or_none,
   316	        number=number_or_none,
   317	        result=entry.result,
   318	        follows=entry.follows,
   319	        by=entry.by,
   320	        record=record,
   321	        notebook=notebook,
   322	        figures=figures,
   323	        files=files,
   324	        log_index=entry.index,
   325	    )
   326	
   327	
   328	def add_log_links(analysis: Analysis, entry: LogEntry) -> None:
   329	    """Add a poe entry's figures and files that its record does not list."""
   330	    _, notebook, figures, files = split_paths(entry.links)
   331	    analysis.notebook = analysis.notebook or notebook
   332	    analysis.figures += [path for path in figures if path not in analysis.figures]
   333	    analysis.files += [path for path in files if path not in analysis.files]
   334	
   335	
   336	def notebook_task(notebook: Optional[str], task_notebooks: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> Optional[str]:
   337	    """Slug of the task whose records name this notebook, else one in the same directory."""
   338	    if not notebook:
   339	        return None
   340	    for slug in sorted(task_notebooks):
   341	        if notebook in task_notebooks[slug]:
   342	            return slug
   343	    directory = Path(notebook).parent
   344	    for slug in sorted(task_notebooks):
   345	        if any(Path(path).parent == directory for path in task_notebooks[slug]):
   346	            return slug
   347	    return None
   348	
   349	
   350	def thread_order(analysis: Analysis):
   351	    """Poe analyses by NN, then untracked work in log order."""
   352	    if analysis.number is not None:
   353	        return (0, analysis.number)
   354	    return (1, analysis.log_index)
   355	
   356	
   357	def arrange(analyses: List[Analysis], entries: List[LogEntry]) -> Page:
   358	    page = Page(record_count=len(analyses), entry_count=len(entries))
   359	    by_key = {analysis.key: analysis for analysis in analyses}
   360	
   361	    untracked_entries = []
   362	    for entry in entries:
   363	        ref = analysis_ref(entry.ref)
   364	        if ref is None:
   365	            untracked_entries.append(analysis_from_log(entry))
   366	        elif ref in by_key:
   367	            add_log_links(by_key[ref], entry)
   368	        else:  # logged by poe but its record is missing
   369	            by_key[ref] = analysis_from_log(entry)
   370	    poe_analyses = sorted(by_key.values(), key=lambda analysis: (analysis.slug, analysis.number))
   371	
   372	    if entries:
   373	        last = entries[-1]
   374	        ref = analysis_ref(last.ref)
   375	        page.latest = by_key[ref] if ref else untracked_entries[-1]
   376	        page.latest.is_latest = True
   377	
   378	    sections: Dict[str, TaskSection] = {}
   379	    task_notebooks: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
   380	    for analysis in poe_analyses:
   381	        sections.setdefault(analysis.slug, TaskSection(slug=analysis.slug))
   382	        if analysis.notebook:
   383	            task_notebooks.setdefault(analysis.slug, []).append(analysis.notebook)
   384	
   385	    # A poe analysis nests under an earlier analysis of the same task; any
   386	    # other parent (unknown, later, another task) makes it a root.
   387	    for analysis in poe_analyses:
   388	        parent = by_key.get(analysis.follows or "")
   389	        if parent is not None and parent.slug == analysis.slug and parent.number < analysis.number:
   390	            parent.children.append(analysis)
   391	        else:
   392	            sections[analysis.slug].roots.append(analysis)
   393	
   394	    for analysis in untracked_entries:
   395	        parent = by_key.get(analysis.follows or "")
   396	        slug = notebook_task(analysis.notebook, task_notebooks)
   397	        if parent is not None:
   398	            parent.children.append(analysis)
   399	        elif slug is not None:
   400	            sections[slug].roots.append(analysis)
   401	        else:
   402	            page.untracked.append(analysis)
   403	
   404	    for analysis in poe_analyses:
   405	        analysis.children.sort(key=thread_order)
   406	    for task in sections.values():
   407	        task.roots.sort(key=thread_order)
   408	    page.tasks = [sections[slug] for slug in sorted(sections)]
   409	    return page
   410	
   411	
   412	def build_page(session_dir: Path) -> Page:
   413	    analyses, skipped_records = load_records(session_dir)
   414	    entries, skipped_entries = load_log(session_dir)
   415	    page = arrange(analyses, entries)
   416	    page.skipped = skipped_records + skipped_entries
   417	    return page
   418	
   419	
   420	# -------------------------------------------------------------------- render
   421

   426	}
   427	@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
   428	  :root {
   429	    --bg: #17171a; --panel: #212125; --text: #e6e6ea; --muted: #9d9da6;
   430	    --border: #3a3a40; --link: #7ab4ff; --latest: #5cc28a; --error: #ff6b6b; --thread: #4a4a52;
   431	  }
   432	}
   433	* { box-sizing: border-box; }
   434	body { margin: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text);
   435	  font: 14px/1.45 -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif; }
   436	main { max-width: 1000px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 16px 16px 64px; }

   441	.meta { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }
   442	.analysis { background: var(--panel); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px;
   443	  padding: 10px 12px; margin: 8px 0; }
   444	.analysis.latest { border-color: var(--latest); border-width: 2px; }
   445	.analysis.dimmed { opacity: 0.55; }
   446	.children { margin: 8px 0 0 12px; padding-left: 12px; border-left: 2px solid var(--thread); }
   447	.head { font-weight: 600; }
   448	.head .number { color: var(--muted); margin-right: 6px; }
   449	.badge { display: inline-block; padding: 0 6px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;
   450	  margin-left: 6px; border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--muted); }
   451	.badge.latest { color: #fff; background: var(--latest); border-color: var(--latest); }
   452	.result { white-space: pre-wrap; overflow-wrap: anywhere; margin: 6px 0; }
   453	.interpretation { margin: 6px 0; color: var(--muted); }
   454	figure { margin: 8px 0; }
   455	figure img { max-width: 100%; height: auto; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 4px; background: #fff; }
   456	figcaption { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
   457	.links { font-size: 12px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
   458	.missing { color: var(--error); }
   459	footer { margin-top: 32px; color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }
   460	"""
   461	
   462	
   463	def escape(text) -> str:
   464	    return html.escape(str(text), quote=True)
   465	
   466	
   467	def page_href(path: str) -> str:
   468	    """Href for a session-relative path, as seen from poe/docs/."""
   469	    if Path(path).is_absolute():
   470	        return quote(path)
   471	    return quote("../../" + path)
   472	
   473	
   474	def file_link(session_dir: Path, path: str, label: str) -> str:
   475	    href = page_href(path)
   476	    if (session_dir / path).exists():
   477	        return f'<a href="{escape(href)}">{escape(label)}</a>'
   478	    return f'<a class="missing" href="{escape(href)}">{escape(label)} (missing)</a>'
   479	
   480	
   481	def render_figure(session_dir: Path, path: str) -> str:
   482	    if not (session_dir / path).exists():
   483	        return f"<p>{file_link(session_dir, path, path)}</p>"
   484	    href = escape(page_href(path))
   485	    return (
   486	        f'<figure><a href="{href}"><img src="{href}" alt="{escape(path)}" loading="lazy"></a>'
   487	        f"<figcaption>{escape(path)}</figcaption></figure>"
   488	    )
   489	
   490	
   491	def render_analysis(session_dir: Path, analysis: Analysis) -> str:
   492	    classes = ["analysis"]
   493	    if analysis.status in DIMMED_STATUSES:
   494	        classes.append("dimmed")
   495	    if analysis.is_latest:
   496	        classes.append("latest")
   497	
   498	    if analysis.number is not None:
   499	        label = f"{analysis.number:02d}"
   500	    else:
   501	        label = "untracked" + (f" ({analysis.by})" if analysis.by else "")
   502	    head = f'<span class="number">{escape(label)}</span>{escape(analysis.question)}'
   503	    if analysis.status:
   504	        head += f'<span class="badge">{escape(analysis.status)}</span>'
   505	    if analysis.is_latest:
   506	        head += '<span class="badge latest">latest</span>'
   507	
   508	    parts = [f'<div class="{" ".join(classes)}" id="{escape(analysis.anchor)}">', f'<div class="head">{head}</div>']
   509	    if analysis.result:
   510	        parts.append(f'<div class="result">{escape(analysis.result)}</div>')
   511	    if analysis.interpretation:
   512	        parts.append(f'<p class="interpretation">{escape(analysis.interpretation)}</p>')
   513	    parts += [render_figure(session_dir, path) for path in analysis.figures]
   514	
   515	    links = []
   516	    if analysis.record:
   517	        links.append("record: " + file_link(session_dir, analysis.record, analysis.record))
   518	    if analysis.notebook:
   519	        links.append("notebook: " + file_link(session_dir, analysis.notebook, analysis.notebook))
   520	    links += [file_link(session_dir, path, path) for path in analysis.files]
   521	    if links:
   522	        parts.append(f'<div class="links">{" · ".join(links)}</div>')
   523	
   524	    if analysis.children:
   525	        children = "".join(render_analysis(session_dir, child) for child in analysis.children)
   526	        parts.append(f'<div class="children">{children}</div>')
   527	    parts.append("</div>")
   528	    return "".join(parts)
   529	
   530	
   531	def render_page(session_dir: Path, page: Page) -> str:
   532	    body = ["<h1>Results</h1>"]
   533	    if not page.tasks and not page.untracked:
   534	        body.append("<p>No analyses yet: no entries in poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md and no poe/&lt;slug&gt;/analysis/ records.</p>")
   535	    else:
   536	        if page.latest is not None:
   537	            body.append(
   538	                f'<p>Latest: <a href="#{escape(page.latest.anchor)}">{escape(page.latest.question)}</a></p>'
   539	            )
   540	        contents = [f'<li><a href="#task-{escape(task.slug)}">{escape(task.slug)}</a></li>' for task in page.tasks]
   541	        if page.untracked:
   542	            contents.append('<li><a href="#untracked-work">Untracked work</a></li>')
   543	        body.append(f"<nav><ul>{''.join(contents)}</ul></nav>")
   544	        for task in page.tasks:
   545	            body.append(f'<section id="task-{escape(task.slug)}"><h2>{escape(task.slug)}</h2>')
   546	            body += [render_analysis(session_dir, analysis) for analysis in task.roots]
   547	            body.append("</section>")
   548	        if page.untracked:
   549	            body.append('<section id="untracked-work"><h2>Untracked work</h2>')
   550	            body += [render_analysis(session_dir, analysis) for analysis in page.untracked]
   551	            body.append("</section>")
   552	    body.append(
   553	        f"<footer>Built from poe/docs/ANALYSIS_LOG.md ({page.entry_count} entries) and "
   554	        f"{page.record_count} analysis records. Skipped {page.skipped} malformed entries.</footer>"
   555	    )
   556	    return (
   557	        "<!DOCTYPE html>\n"
   558	        '<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">'
   559	        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">'
   560	        "<title>poe results</title>"
   561	        f'<meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark"><style>{CSS}</style></head>'
   562	        f"<body><main>{''.join(body)}</main></body></html>\n"
   563	    )
   564	
   565	
   566	def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
   567	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Write poe/docs/all_analysis.html for a session directory.")
   568	    parser.add_argument("session_dir", nargs="?", default=".", help="the directory holding poe/ (default: .)")
   569	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   570	    session_dir = Path(args.session_dir)
   571	    if not session_dir.is_dir():
   572	        print(f"poe_results: session directory not found: {session_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
   573	        return 1
   574	
   575	    try:
   576	        page = build_page(session_dir)
   577	    except OSError as error:
   578	        print(f"poe_results: could not read the analyses: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
   579	        return 1
   580	
   581	    page_path = session_dir / PAGE_PATH
   582	    try:
   583	        page_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   584	        page_path.write_text(render_page(session_dir, page), encoding="utf-8")
   585	    except OSError as error:
   586	        print(f"poe_results: could not write the results page: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
   587	        return 1
   588	    print(page_path)
   589	    return 0
   590	
   591	
   592	if __name__ == "__main__":
   593	    sys.exit(main())

#12 · 2026-11-20T22:30:49.051Z · thinking
I'm noting that the image syntax `![umap](...)` in 02-normalise needs handling, since the subset doesn't support images. Rather than leave the leading `!` behind as a stray character, I'll turn the image markdown into a plain link with the alt text as the label, and let the regex consume the optional `!`. The figures are rendered separately anyway, so that keeps things simple. I'm weighing whether adding a new fixture record would shift the record count and the nesting tests, so I'm leaning toward extending the existing clustering/01 Result with richer markdown instead, since that keeps the counts stable. I'm settling on how the Interpretation field should be parsed: taking the first block up to a blank line and keeping its line breaks so a list survives, while the existing test still holds. I'm also deciding to wrap the rendered output in an interpretation div, since it will now be block HTML rather than a paragraph. I'm now parsing the markdown line by line: skipping blank lines, recognising tables by a pipe-containing line followed by a separator row, and grouping list items into nested blocks based on indentation. I'm sketching how a table nested inside a list item should be handled: when an indented pipe line is followed by a separator row, it becomes a table within the current item rather than ending the list. I'm leaning toward a recursive block parser that treats each item as text plus child blocks, so nested lists and tables can sit under the same bullet. I'm writing the block parser as a loop that skips blanks, picks out tables and list items, and otherwise collects paragraph lines. show the rest (3,152 more characters)
I'm weighing how to handle a list that runs into a nested table or sub-list. When a line is indented deeper than the current bullet, it should go under that item as child content instead of ending the list, and a non-indented line ends it.

I'm working out how the block parser should handle a table that sits under a list item, and settling on dedenting the child lines and recursing so the item keeps its text and nested content together.

I'm treating child lines as nested content: dedenting them by their common indent so a nested bullet or table parses at the top level, then recursing. Each level shrinks the input, so recursion stays bounded, and I'll wrap the renderer in a fallback that escapes everything as plain paragraphs if anything goes wrong. Tabs get expanded first so indentation stays consistent, and a line only counts as nested when it's indented deeper than its bullet.

I'm working out the table detection rule: a line with a pipe followed by a separator row of dashes, with optional colons, makes a table. I'll strip the outer pipes and split the remaining cells, padding short rows to the header's column count. Alignment markers can be ignored for now.

I'm working out how table detection should work: the header line has to contain a pipe and be followed by a separator row of dashes, both with a pipe, and I'll pad any short rows to the header's column count.

I'm working out how inline text gets escaped and formatted: splitting out code spans first so their contents stay literal, then applying bold, italic and link handling to the remaining segments, and unescaping URLs before building the hrefs.

I'm working out how inline text gets escaped and formatted, using placeholders for code spans and links so that bold and italic markers don't mangle URLs, and I'm leaning toward supporting only `*italic*` to avoid trouble with underscores in file names.

I'm treating the link and image rules in the inline renderer: an image becomes a link with its alt text as the label, and a link target with a scheme such as javascript: is rendered as plain text rather than a link. For local paths I'm splitting off any #fragment so it isn't percent-encoded along with the path.

I'm making sure the inline renderer protects code spans, bold, and italic from interfering with each other, and checking the output for leftover double asterisks.

I'm settling on keeping line breaks inside paragraphs as `<br>`, since the records were written line by line and the previous display preserved them. For list item continuation lines, I'm leaning toward joining with a space as wrapped text.

I'm finishing the record-rendering changes: the interpretation will keep its line breaks via a first-block parser, so the existing test still passes. I'm also adding an escaped `<script>` sample to the fixture so the no-script check covers it, and switching the result styling to normal paragraph spacing instead of pre-wrap.

Still writing the stylesheet: adding table and code-span styles, plus a light and dark code background.

- Read: `skills/poe/SKILL.md` (Notebook rules, Analyse mode step 5), `agents/phase-worker.md`, `README.md` (Analysis section, line ~91 Notebook rules).
- May change: `skills/poe/SKILL.md`, `agents/phase-worker.md`, `README.md`.
- Work:
  - SKILL.md Notebook rules: terse bullets: figures `.svg` only (no PNG), `bbox_inches="tight"`; setup cell at notebook top (snippet from plan Assumptions, tagged like other poe cells; add when missing); size caps (4x3 default, ≤3.5 in wide small/square, ≤7.2 in multi-panel, never larger, set via `figsize`); dense artists `rasterized=True`.
  - SKILL.md Analyse step 5: also check figure paths in record end `.svg`; else send back to worker.
  - phase-worker.md: same rules (or explicit pointer if it already defers to SKILL.md Notebook rules); example `fig.png` → `fig.svg`.
  - README.md: extend Notebook rules bullet with one sentence on figure rules.
- Non-goals: code changes, converting old PNGs, `.mplstyle`/helper module, changing other modes.
- Verify:
  - `grep -n "svg.fonttype" skills/poe/SKILL.md agents/phase-worker.md`
  - `grep -n "fig.png\|\.png" agents/phase-worker.md skills/poe/SKILL.md` → no PNG examples left as recommended output.
  - `python3 -m unittest discover tests` (unchanged pass).
- Done when: both files state all 4 rules + snippet; Analyse step 5 has `.svg` check; README mentions rules; output style terse (SKILL.md Output style).

# results-md-figures

## Decisions
- Results page renders record markdown via small stdlib subset renderer in `poe_results.py` (paragraphs, nested bullets, tables, bold/italic, inline code, links); replaces escaped pre-wrap text. Why: page stays stdlib-only, no JS, self-contained.
- Figure size: default 4x3 in per single plot; small/square ≤3.5 in wide (e.g. scatter 3.5x3.5); multi-panel ≤7.2 in wide (double column); never larger. Why: plots drop into journal panels without resizing.
- Figure font: Arial 7 pt at final size; `svg.fonttype='none'` keeps text editable. Why: journal-typical size; Arial present in Inkscape almost everywhere.
- Figure rules live in Notebook rules (SKILL.md + phase-worker.md): one rcParams setup cell at notebook top (snippet given), figsize per plot, save `.svg` only. Why: notebook self-contained, reruns anywhere; no plugin-path dependency.
- Confirmed by user 2026-11-20.

## Objective

- Results page (`poe/docs/all_analysis.html`) renders record markdown properly (screenshot: literal `**`, `- `, `| table |`).
- Every analysis plot is paper-panel-ready: SVG, editable text, panel size.

## Scope

- `skills/poe/scripts/poe_results.py`: stdlib markdown-subset renderer for `## Result` and Interpretation; CSS for lists, tables, code.
- `tests/test_poe_results.py` + `tests/fixtures/results/`: record with nested bullets, table, bold, code, link.
- `skills/poe/SKILL.md` Notebook rules + `agents/phase-worker.md`: figure rules + setup-cell snippet; Analyse step 5 also checks figure paths end `.svg`.
- `README.md`: one line on figure rules, if README lists notebook rules.

Excluded:
- Converting existing PNGs or re-running old notebooks (forward only).
- Full CommonMark (headings inside sections, blockquotes, HTML passthrough, footnotes).
- Shipped `.mplstyle` / helper module; non-matplotlib libs beyond "same rules apply".
- Changes to `poe_report.py`.

## Assumptions

- Markdown subset: paragraphs; `-`/`*`/`1.` lists nested by indent (2+ spaces); pipe tables with `---` separator row; `**bold**`, `*italic*`, `` `code` ``, `[text](url)`. Anything else → escaped text in a paragraph. Everything escaped before inline markup applied (no HTML injection).
- Link targets that are session-relative paths → rewritten via existing `page_href`; `http(s)` kept.
- Interpretation: still first block only (paragraph or list), rendered with same renderer.
- Figures detection unchanged (`.svg` already an image extension); SVG inlined via `<img>`, `max-width: 100%`.
- Setup cell (top of notebook, tagged like other poe cells):
  ```python
  import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  plt.rcParams.update({
      "svg.fonttype": "none",
      "font.family": "sans-serif",
      "font.sans-serif": ["Arial", "Helvetica", "DejaVu Sans"],
      "font.size": 7,
      "axes.titlesize": 7, "axes.labelsize": 7,
      "xtick.labelsize": 7, "ytick.labelsize": 7, "legend.fontsize": 7,
      "figure.figsize": (4, 3),
  })
  ```
- Save: `fig.savefig("<name>.svg", bbox_inches="tight")`; no PNG. Dense artists (scatters ≳10k points, images) `rasterized=True`.
- Size caps: default 4x3 in; small/square ≤3.5 in wide (e.g. 3.5x3.5); multi-panel ≤7.2 in wide; never larger. Set per plot via `figsize`.
- seaborn/pandas plotting inherit rcParams → covered. Plotly etc. not used by default; if used, same SVG/size/font rules in spirit.
- Existing notebook lacking setup cell → worker adds it at top when it next appends.

## Approach

- `poe_results.py`: add `render_markdown(text, session_dir) -> str` (block pass: tables, lists via indent stack, paragraphs) + `render_inline(text)` (escape, then code → bold → italic → links). Replace `<div class="result">{escape(...)}` and interpretation `<p>` with it. Drop `white-space: pre-wrap`; add CSS for `ul/ol`, `table` (borders, padding, `overflow-x:auto` wrapper), `code`.
- SKILL.md Notebook rules: new bullets: figures SVG only; setup cell snippet; size caps; rasterize dense artists. Keep terse.
- phase-worker.md: same rules (or point to SKILL.md Notebook rules if worker already reads them); change example `fig.png` → `fig.svg`.

## Acceptance criteria

- Fixture record with nested bullets, table, bold, code, link renders as `<ul>` nesting, `<table>`, `<strong>`, `<code>`, `<a>`; no literal `**` or `|` row text in output.
- `<script>` in a record → escaped, not emitted raw.
- Byte-identical output on rerun; existing tests pass (update ones asserting old pre-wrap output).
- SKILL.md + phase-worker.md state: SVG only, `svg.fonttype: none`, Arial 7 pt, size caps, setup cell snippet.
- Manual: rebuild page on a real project with the screenshot record (`FLLQHLPLV`) → bullets/table render cleanly, light + dark.

## Risks

- Agent-written markdown is loose (odd indents, tables without leading `|`) → renderer misparses. Mitigation: tolerant parse; fallback = escaped paragraph, never crash.
- Arial missing on cluster/Linux → matplotlib falls back to DejaVu with warning; SVG still names Arial-first stack, Inkscape on Mac uses Arial. Accepted.
- Workers ignore rules → plots still big/PNG. Mitigation: rule in both SKILL.md and phase-worker.md; orchestrator Analyse step 5 checks figure paths end `.svg`.

## Validation

- `python3 -m unittest discover tests`
- `python3 skills/poe/scripts/poe_results.py tests/fixtures/results` → open page, check rendering light + dark.
- Grep: `grep -n "svg.fonttype" skills/poe/SKILL.md agents/phase-worker.md`.
